Triton kernel single-pass for H power-of-two <= 256, Cin maybe up to 512. Steps:
```
@triton.jit
def final_fused_kernel(out_ptr, gate_ptr, w_ptr, out_ptr_res, M, H: tl.constexpr, C: tl.constexpr, eps, BLOCK_M: tl.constexpr, BLOCK_H: tl.constexpr, BLOCK_C: tl.constexpr, NUM_C: tl.constexpr, H_POW2: tl.constexpr):
  pid_m = tl.program_id(0)
  offs_m = pid_m*BLOCK_M + tl.arange(0,BLOCK_M)
  offs_m_mask = offs_m < M
  offs_h = tl.arange(0, BLOCK_H)
  h_mask = offs_h < H
  mask = offs_m_mask[:,None] & h_mask[None,:]
  out = tl.load(out_ptr + offs_m[:,None]*H + offs_h[None,:], mask=mask, other=0).to(tl.float32)
  gate = tl.load(gate_ptr + ...).to(f32)
  # LayerNorm over H: if H not equal BLOCK_H, sums include zeros okay, divide by H.
  mean = tl.sum(out, axis=1) / H
  var = tl.sum(out*out, axis=1) / H - mean*mean
  rstd = 1 / tl.sqrt(var + eps)
  norm = (out - mean[:,None]) * rstd[:,None]
  # apply to_out_norm weight? Wait to_out_norm = nn.LayerNorm(hidden_dim) includes weight and bias! I forgot.
```

Pseudo:
```
@triton.jit
def final_kernel(out_ptr, gate_ptr, nw_ptr, nb_ptr, wout_ptr, y_ptr, M, H: tl.constexpr, C: tl.constexpr, eps, BLOCK_M: tl.constexpr, BLOCK_H: tl.constexpr, BLOCK_C: tl.constexpr, NUM_H_CHUNKS: tl.constexpr, NUM_C_CHUNKS: tl.constexpr):
  pid = tl.program_id(0)
  offs_m = pid*BLOCK_M + arange(0,BLOCK_M)
  m_mask = offs_m < M
  offs_h = arange(0, BLOCK_H)
  h_mask = offs_h < BLOCK_H? chunk h offsets handle.
  sum = zeros([BLOCK_M], f32); sumsq = zeros([BLOCK_M])
  for hc in range(0, H, BLOCK_H):
     h_off = hc + offs_h
     hmask = h_off < H
     mask2 = m_mask[:,None] & hmask[None,:]
     val = load(out_ptr + offs_m[:,None]*H + h_off[None,:], mask=mask2, other=0).to(f32)
     sum += tl.sum(val, axis=1)
     sumsq += tl.sum(val*val, axis=1)
  mean = sum/H
  var = sumsq/H - mean*mean
  rstd = 1/sqrt(var+eps)
  # For each c chunk:
  for cc in range(0, C, BLOCK_C):
     c_off = cc + arange(0,BLOCK_C)
     cmask = c_off < C
     acc = zeros([BLOCK_M, BLOCK_C], f32)
     for hc in range(0, H, BLOCK_H):
        h_off = hc + offs_h
        hmask2 = h_off < H
        mask2 = m_mask[:,None] & hmask2[None,:]
        val = load(out_ptr + ..., mask=mask2, other=0).to(f32)
        gate = load(gate_ptr + ..., mask=mask2, other=0).to(f32)
        # norm weight/bias chunk:
        nw = load(nw_ptr + h_off, mask=hmask2, other=0)
        nb = load(nb_ptr + h_off, mask=hmask2, other=0)
        norm = (val - mean[:,None]) * rstd[:,None] * nw[None,:] + nb[None,:]
        xh = norm * gate
        # weight chunk W_t [BLOCK_H, BLOCK_C]: wout[c,h] -> ptr = wout_ptr + c_off[None,:]*H + h_off[:,None]
        wmask = hmask2[:,None] & cmask[None,:]
        w = load(wout_ptr + c_off[None,:]*H + h_off[:,None], mask=wmask, other=0) # f32
        # dot. Use xh f32 and w f32 with tf32? But xh [M,BH], w [BH,Cchunk]. If BLOCK_H maybe 128. Good.
        acc += tl.dot(xh, w, input_precision="tf32")
     # store acc [M,BC] to y
     store(y_ptr + offs_m[:,None]*C + c_off[None,:], acc, mask=m_mask[:,None]&cmask[None,:])
```

Single-pass kernel code details:
```
@triton.jit
def _final_single_kernel(out_ptr, gate_ptr, gate_stride, lw_ptr, lb_ptr, wout_ptr, y_ptr,
                         M, H: tl.constexpr, C: tl.constexpr, eps,
                         BLOCK_M: tl.constexpr, BLOCK_C: tl.constexpr):
    pid = tl.program_id(0)
    offs_m = pid * BLOCK_M + tl.arange(0, BLOCK_M)
    mm = offs_m < M
    offs_h = tl.arange(0, H)
    # load
    out = tl.load(out_ptr + offs_m[:, None] * H + offs_h[None, :], mask=mm[:, None], other=0.0).to(tl.float32)
    gate = tl.load(gate_ptr + offs_m[:, None] * gate_stride + offs_h[None, :], mask=mm[:, None], other=0.0).to(tl.float32)
    # norm weight/bias
    lw = tl.load(lw_ptr + offs_h).to(tl.float32)
    lb = tl.load(lb_ptr + offs_h).to(tl.float32)
    mean = tl.sum(out, axis=1) / H
    var = tl.sum(out * out, axis=1) / H - mean * mean
    rstd = 1.0 / tl.sqrt(var + eps)
    x = (out - mean[:, None]) * rstd[:, None] * lw[None, :] + lb[None, :]
    x = x * gate
    # loop c
    for c0 in range(0, C, BLOCK_C):
        offs_c = c0 + tl.arange(0, BLOCK_C)
        cm = offs_c < C
        w = tl.load(wout_ptr + c_off? ... )
        acc = tl.dot(x, w, input_precision="tf32")
        tl.store(y_ptr + offs_m[:, None] * C + offs_c[None, :], acc, mask=mm[:, None] & cm[None, :])
```
Need `tl.dot` with x f32 w f32. If H=64, K=64; okay. If H=128. If C maybe 384, offs_c [0:128], w shape [H,128]. w pointers: wout [C,H] (out_features C, in H). For c row, h col: pointer = wout_ptr + offs_c[None, :] * H + offs_h[:, None]. Load mask cm. Good.
One issue: `range(0, C, BLOCK_C)` inside JIT with C constexpr okay? If C=128, unroll one. If C=384, three. If C not multiple, mask last.
For dot, if C last chunk < BLOCK_C, w has zeros for invalid c; output invalid not stored. Good.
For `x` f32, dot tf32. Is tf32 allowed with f32 and N (C block) maybe 128. yes.
`tl.sum(out * out, axis=1)` computes sum of squares. For heavy values, var could be negative small due to cancellation; use `tl.maximum(var,0)`? To avoid sqrt negative. `var = tl.maximum(var, 0.0)`.
LayerNorm default in PyTorch: eps inside sqrt. Good.

Generic kernel code:
```
@triton.jit
def _final_chunk_kernel(out_ptr, gate_ptr, gate_stride, lw_ptr, lb_ptr, wout_ptr, y_ptr, M, H: tl.constexpr, C: tl.constexpr, eps, BLOCK_M: tl.constexpr, BLOCK_H: tl.constexpr, BLOCK_C: tl.constexpr):
  pid...
  offs_m...
  mm...
  sum = tl.zeros([BLOCK_M], f32); sumsq
  for h0 in range(0, H, BLOCK_H):
    offs_h = h0 + tl.arange(0, BLOCK_H)
    hm = offs_h < H
    mask = mm[:,None] & hm[None,:]
    val = tl.load(out_ptr + offs_m[:,None]*H + offs_h[None,:], mask=mask, other=0.).to(f32)
    sum += tl.sum(val, axis=1)
    sumsq += tl.sum(val*val, axis=1)
  mean...
  for c0 in range(0, C, BLOCK_C):
    offs_c = c0 + tl.arange(0, BLOCK_C)
    cm = offs_c < C
    acc = zeros([BLOCK_M,BLOCK_C])
    for h0 in range(0, H, BLOCK_H):
       ... load val, gate, lw, lb
       x = ... * gate
       w = tl.load(wout_ptr + offs_c[None,:]*H + offs_h[:,None], mask=hm[:,None]&cm[None,:], other=0.)
       acc += tl.dot(x, w, input_precision="tf32")
    store acc
```

Host function:
```
def _ln_to_bf16(x, M, C, weight, bias, eps, dtype=bf16):
    y = torch.empty((M,C), dtype=dt, device=x.device)
    BLOCK_C = triton.next_power_of_two(C)
    if BLOCK_C <=128: BLOCK_M=128
    elif BLOCK_C<=256: BLOCK_M=64
    elif BLOCK_C<=512: BLOCK_M=32
    else: BLOCK_M=16
    grid=(triton.cdiv(M,BLOCK_M),)
    _ln_to_bf16_kernel[grid](x, y, weight, bias, M, C=C, eps=eps, BLOCK_M=..., BLOCK_C=..., num_warps=4)
    return y
```

```
@triton.jit
def _precompute_tri_kernel(
    x_ptr, mask_ptr,
    wl_ptr, wr_ptr, wlg_ptr, wrg_ptr, wog_ptr,
    lw_ptr, lb_ptr,
    L_ptr, R_ptr, G_ptr,
    N, C: tl.constexpr, eps,
    H: tl.constexpr, P: tl.constexpr, BC: tl.constexpr, HAS_MASK: tl.constexpr,
):
    pid_k = tl.program_id(0)
    i = tl.program_id(1)
    b = tl.program_id(2)
    offs_k = pid_k * P + tl.arange(0, P)
    k_mask = offs_k < N
    x_base = x_ptr + (b * N * N + i * N) * C
    # LN
    csum = tl.zeros((P,), dtype=tl.float32)
    cssq = tl.zeros((P,), dtype=tl.float32)
    for c0 in range(0, C, BC):
        offs_c = c0 + tl.arange(0, BC)
        cmask = (offs_c[:, None] < C) & k_mask[None, :]
        xchunk = tl.load(x_base + offs_k[None, :] * C + offs_c[:, None], mask=cmask, other=0.0).to(tl.float32)
        csum += tl.sum(xchunk, axis=0)
        cssq += tl.sum(xchunk * xchunk, axis=0)
    mean = csum / C
    var = cssq / C - mean*mean
    rstd = 1.0 / tl.sqrt(var + eps)
    # accs
    h_offs = tl.arange(0, H)
    accL = tl.zeros((H, P), dtype=tl.float32)
    accR = tl.zeros((H, P), dtype=tl.float32)
    accLG = tl.zeros((H, P), dtype=tl.float32)
    accRG = tl.zeros((H, P), dtype=tl.float32)
    accOG = tl.zeros((H, P), dtype=tl.float32)
    for c0 in range(0, C, BC):
       offs_c = c0 + tl.arange(0, BC)
       cmask2 = (offs_c[:, None] < C) & k_mask[None, :]
       xchunk = tl.load(...).to(f32)
       normw = tl.load(lw_ptr + offs_c, mask=offs_c < C, other=0.0)
       normb = tl.load(lb_ptr + offs_c, mask=offs_c < C, other=0.0)
       xnorm = (xchunk - mean[None,:]) * rstd[None,:] * normw[None,:] + normb[None,:]
       xb = xnorm.to(tl.bfloat16)
       # weight chunks
       # w shape [H,C], load [H,BC]
       wmask = (h_offs[:,None] < H) & (offs_c[None,:] < C)  # H exact so h true
       wh = tl.load(wl_ptr + h_offs[:,None]*C + offs_c[None,:], mask=wmask, other=0).to(tl.bfloat16)
       accL = tl.dot(wh, xb) + accL
       ... five
    if HAS_MASK:
       m = tl.load(mask_ptr + (b*N*N + i*N + offs_k), mask=k_mask, other=0.0).to(tl.float32)
       m_none = m[None,:]
    else:
       m_none = None? Can't assign None to [H,P]? We can if/else around stores.
```

Let's write single final kernel with `BLOCK_H` maybe 128.
```
@triton.jit
def _final_single_kernel(out_ptr, gate_ptr, lw_ptr, lb_ptr, wout_ptr, y_ptr, M, H: tl.constexpr, C: tl.constexpr, eps, BLOCK_M: tl.constexpr, BLOCK_H: tl.constexpr, BLOCK_C: tl.constexpr):
    pid=...
    offs_m=...
    mm=offs_m<M
    offs_h=tl.arange(0,BLOCK_H); hm=offs_h<H
    omask=mm[:,None]&hm[None,:]
    out=tl.load(out_ptr+offs_m[:,None]*H+offs_h[None,:], mask=omask, other=0.).to(f32)
    gate=tl.load(gate_ptr+offs_m[:,None]*H+offs_h[None,:], mask=omask, other=0.).to(f32)
    lw=tl.load(lw_ptr+offs_h, mask=hm, other=0.)
    lb=tl.load(lb_ptr+offs_h, mask=hm, other=0.)
    mean=tl.sum(out,axis=1)/H
    var=tl.sum(out*out,axis=1)/H-mean*mean
    var=tl.maximum(var,0.)
    rstd=1./tl.sqrt(var+eps)
    x=(out-mean[:,None])*rstd[:,None]*lw[None,:]+lb[None,:]
    x=x*gate
    # maybe zero invalid h
    x = x * hm[None,:].to(tl.float32)
    for c0 in range(0,C,BLOCK_C):
       offs_c=...
       cm=offs_c<C
       wmask=hm[:,None]&cm[None,:]
       w=tl.load(wout_ptr+offs_c[None,:]*H+offs_h[:,None], mask=wmask, other=0.).to(f32)
       acc=tl.dot(x, w, input_precision="tf32")
       tl.store(y_ptr+offs_m[:,None]*C+offs_c[None,:], acc, mask=mm[:,None]&cm[None,:])
```
For `x` invalid h, because hm false, out/gate 0, lw/lb 0, x = (0-mean)*rstd*0+0=0. Good; multiply hm not needed but okay. However `mean` for padded H? H actual, not BLOCK_H. Good.
If H not multiple of BLOCK_H? Only if BLOCK_H next power >H. For H=128 exact. If H=100, BLOCK_H=128, sum out includes zeros; divide H=100. Padded h normalization: out=0, lw=0, lb=0, x=0? But `(0-mean)*rstd*0=0`, good.
If H<16, BLOCK_H=16. Dot K=16. Good.

Now PyTorch separate fallback for H != 128:
```
def _torch_precompute_separate(x, mask, weights, B,N,H,C, device, dt):
   M=B*N*N
   x_flat = x.reshape(M,C).contiguous()
   x_bf = _ln_to_bf16(x_flat, C, norm_w, norm_b)
   del x_flat
   # helper get weight_t
   def wt(name): return weights[name].to(dt).t().contiguous()  # [C,H]
   wl=wt('left_proj.weight')
   left = torch.matmul(x_bf, wl)
   lg = torch.matmul(x_bf, wt('left_gate.weight'))
   torch.sigmoid_(lg)
   torch.mul_(left, lg)
   del lg
   wr=...
   right=...
   rg...
   del rg
   wog=...
   og = torch.matmul(x_bf, wog)
   torch.sigmoid_(og)
   del x_bf, weights? 
   if mask is not None:
      m = mask.to(dt).reshape(M,1)
      left *= m; right *= m
   Lh = left.view(B,N,N,H).permute(0,3,1,2).contiguous()
   del left
   Rh = right...
   del right
   return Lh, Rh, og
```

Kernel helper `_ln_to_bf16_kernel`:
```
@triton.jit
def _ln_to_bf16_kernel(x_ptr, y_ptr, w_ptr, b_ptr, M, C: tl.constexpr, eps, BLOCK_M: tl.constexpr, BLOCK_C: tl.constexpr):
   pid = tl.program_id(0)
   offs_m = pid*BLOCK_M + tl.arange(0,BLOCK_M)
   mm = offs_m < M
   offs_c = tl.arange(0,BLOCK_C)
   cm = offs_c < C
   mask = mm[:,None] & cm[None,:]
   x = tl.load(x_ptr + offs_m[:,None]*C + offs_c[None,:], mask=mask, other=0.).to(tl.float32)
   mean = tl.sum(x, axis=1)/C
   var = tl.sum(x*x, axis=1)/C - mean*mean
   var = tl.maximum(var, 0.)
   rstd = 1.0/tl.sqrt(var+eps)
   w = tl.load(w_ptr + offs_c, mask=cm, other=0.)
   b = tl.load(b_ptr + offs_c, mask=cm, other=0.)
   y = (x - mean[:,None])*rstd[:,None]*w[None,:] + b[None,:]
   tl.store(y_ptr + offs_m[:,None]*C + offs_c[None,:], y.to(tl.bfloat16), mask=mask)
```
If C not multiple (BLOCK_C> C) w/b pad 0, y invalid stored pad masked. If C=128, BLOCK_C=128.

Let's outline final code:
```
import torch
import triton
import triton.language as tl

@triton.jit
def _ln_to_bf16_kernel(...): ...

@triton.jit
def _precompute_kernel(...): ...

@triton.jit
def _final_single_kernel(...): ...

@triton.jit
def _final_chunk_kernel(...): ...

def _ln_to_bf16(...): ...

def _precompute_torch_separate(...): ...

def custom_kernel(data):
    # unpack
    # x = data[0] ...
    if not x.is_contiguous(): x=x.contiguous()
    if mask is not None: mask=mask.contiguous()
    B,N,_,C = x.shape
    H = config.get('hidden_dim', weights['left_proj.weight'].shape[0])
    M=B*N*N
    dt=torch.bfloat16
    eps=1e-5
    # weights f32 for norms
    nw = weights['norm.weight'].to(torch.float32).contiguous()
    nb = weights['norm.bias'].to(torch.float32).contiguous()
    tow = weights['to_out.weight'].to(torch.float32).contiguous()
    tnw = weights['to_out_norm.weight'].to(torch.float32).contiguous()
    tnb = weights['to_out_norm.bias'].to(torch.float32).contiguous()
    if H == 128:
       # precompute weights bf16
       wl = weights['left_proj.weight'].to(dt).contiguous()
       ...
       Lh = torch.empty((B,H,N,N), dtype=dt, device=x.device)
       Rh = ...
       G = torch.empty((M,H), dtype=dt, device=x.device)
       P=32; BC=128
       grid=(triton.cdiv(N,P), N, B)
       mask_arg = mask if mask is not None else x
       _precompute_kernel[grid](x, mask_arg, wl, wr, wlg, wrg, wog, nw, nb, Lh, Rh, G, N, C=C, eps=eps, H=H, P=P, BC=BC, HAS_MASK=(mask is not None), num_warps=8)
       del wl,...
    else:
       Lh, Rh, G = _precompute_torch_separate(...)
    # bmm
    LH = Lh.view(B*H, N, N)
    RH = Rh.view(B*H, N, N)
    with torch.cuda.amp.autocast? no
    out_h = torch.bmm(LH, RH.transpose(1,2))
    del LH, RH, Lh, Rh
    out = out_h.view(B,H,N,N).permute(0,2,3,1).contiguous()
    del out_h
    # final
    y = torch.empty((B,N,N,C), dtype=torch.float32, device=x.device)
    BLOCK_C = 128 if C >= 128 else max(16, triton.next_power_of_two(C))
    BLOCK_M = 64
    grid=(triton.cdiv(M,BLOCK_M),)
    if H <= 128:
       BLOCK_H = max(16, triton.next_power_of_two(H))
       _final_single_kernel[grid](out, G, tnw, tnb, tow, y, M, H=H, C=C, eps=eps, BLOCK_M=BLOCK_M, BLOCK_H=BLOCK_H, BLOCK_C=BLOCK_C, num_warps=4)
    else:
       BLOCK_H = 128
       _final_chunk_kernel[grid](out, G, tnw, tnb, tow, y, M, H=H, C=C, eps=eps, BLOCK_M=BLOCK_M, BLOCK_H=BLOCK_H, BLOCK_C=BLOCK_C, num_warps=4)
    return y.view(B,N,N,C)
```

`G` is 2D [M,H] contiguous.

After c loop, pointwise. `lsig = tl.sigmoid(accLG)`; `rsig`; `osig`.
Mask: if HAS_MASK: `m = tl.load(mask_ptr + b*N*N + i*N + offs_k, mask=k_mask, other=0.0).to(tl.float32)`; else `m = tl.full((P,), 1.0, tl.float32)`. Then `mrow = m[None, :]`. `L = accL * lsig * mrow`. `R = accR * rsig * mrow`. `OG = osig`.
Could multiply mask before sigmoid? no difference.
Store L/R. For L, `L_ptrs = L_ptr + b*H*N*N + h_offs[:,None]*N*N + i*N + offs_k[None,:]`. To reduce repeated compute, base. `L_base = L_ptr + (b*H*N*N + i*N)`. But b*H*N*N dynamic. Good.
`tl.store(L_ptrs, L.to(tl.bfloat16), mask=k_mask[None, :])`. If H=128 all h. If H not 128? host only H=128, but if H=64 maybe h full. good.
G store: `OGT = tl.trans(OG).to(tl.bfloat16)`; `G_base = G_ptr + (b*N*N + i*N)*H`; `G_ptrs = G_base + offs_k[:,None]*H + h_offs[None,:]`; store mask `k_mask[:,None]`.
If P=32, trans [H,P]->[P,H]. Good.

Potential: `tl.trans` on 2D tensor with shape (128,32) okay. If H not power2? H=128. Good.

```
elif len(data)==3:
   if isinstance(data[1], dict): x, weights, config = data; mask=None
   else: x, mask, config = data; weights = data[1]? no, missing weights. Maybe (x, mask, weights/config?) Not.
```
Better follow skeleton: 4 or 3 with dict second. If len 3 and data[1] dict, mask none. If len 3 and data[2] dict? (x, mask, config) missing weights impossible. So okay.
Could make:
```
if len(data)==4: ...
elif len(data)==3:
   if isinstance(data[1], dict): x, weights, config = data; mask=None
   else: x, mask, weights = data; config = {}? no config missing? not.
else: ...
```
Given problem, use 4/3.

Config may not have hidden_dim; if weights left_proj weight shape 0? no.

Potential hidden issue: `weights` dict values may be on CPU? Problem says tensors on cuda device. If not, `.to(device)` maybe. We use `.to(dtype)` but not device; if CPU, kernel on GPU fail. Could `.to(device=device, dtype=...)`. Let's do for weights: define `dev = x.device`; for each `.to(dtype=..., device=dev)`. For small. Helper `_to_w`:
```
def _w(name, dtype=torch.float32): return weights[name].to(dtype=dtype, device=dev).contiguous()
```
In precompute H=128, weights bf16 with device. Norm f32. to_out f32.
In fallback, same.
For mask, if not on device? `mask = mask.to(device=dev)` maybe. Input x on device. Use `mask.to(dt, device=dev)`.
For weights in `.to(dt)` no device, likely same. Add device to be safe.
```
wl = weights['left_proj.weight'].to(dt, device=dev).contiguous()
```
Similarly norm.
This avoids CPU issue.

Potential hidden issue: `torch.empty` dtype bf16 on x.device. good.

Now, final code. Ensure no annotations requiring `input_t`.

Let's produce final answer. We'll include docstring at top.

One more thought: In precompute kernel, we compute layer norm for each x tile (one i, P k). The same x row (i,k) normalized. Good. But out_gate G position (i,k) corresponds to x[b,i,k]. In final, output pair is (i,j). out_gate for output (i,j) should be based on x[b,i,j], not x[b,i,k]? Wait! This is important. In reference, out_gate = out_gate(x) where x is [B,N,N,C]. For each output position (i,j), gate = out_gate[x[b,i,j]]. In our precompute tile over x second dimension k; we store G at row (i,k). That's correct: k plays role of second sequence index. In final, row p (i,j) reads G row (i,j). Good.
Left projection uses x[b,i,k], right uses x[b,j,k]. Our Lh [b,h,i,k] from x position (i,k). Rh [b,h,j,k] from x position (j,k). Since precompute computes both L and R for every x position (first_dim, second_dim). For Rh, the first_dim is the output j coordinate; for Lh first_dim is i. Same x positions. Good. Mask G not masked.

But wait, mask application: For left[b,i,k] mask[b,i,k]; right[b,j,k] mask[b,j,k]. In precompute tile first dim=i, second=k. We apply same mask[b,i,k] to both L and R. For R in this tile, first dim is also the row coordinate for right (call j). It uses mask[b,j,k]. Since the tile coordinate first_dim is j, correct. Good.

Now, in fallback separate, `left` and `right` both computed from x_flat rows (first_dim, second_dim). We apply same mask_flat (first_dim,second_dim) to left/right. For right, first_dim is j, correct. Good.

Potential subtle: The right projection in reference `right = self.right_proj(x)`; then in einsum used as `right[b,j,k,d]`. So our right tensor layout uses first_dim as j, second as k. Good.

Now, final Lh/Rh bmm: Lh [b,h,i,k], Rh [b,h,j,k]. bmm L @ R^T gives out[b,h,i,j]=sum_k L[i,k] R[j,k]. Good.

Now, all good.

Let's write final code. Need ensure code block only.
</think>

```python
import torch
import triton
import triton.language as tl


# Fused Triton/PyTorch implementation of outgoing TriMul.
# Fast path for hidden_dim == 128: one Triton kernel fuses input LayerNorm,
# all five projections, gating, and mask application, and writes the two
# pair-tensors directly in [B, H, N, N] layout for an efficient bmm.
# A second Triton kernel fuses the final LayerNorm, out-gate, and output
# linear projection. A PyTorch fallback handles other hidden dims.


@triton.jit
def _ln_to_bf16_kernel(
    x_ptr,
    y_ptr,
    w_ptr,
    b_ptr,
    M,
    C: tl.constexpr,
    eps,
    BLOCK_M: tl.constexpr,
    BLOCK_C: tl.constexpr,
):
    pid = tl.program_id(0)
    offs_m = pid * BLOCK_M + tl.arange(0, BLOCK_M)
    offs_c = tl.arange(0, BLOCK_C)

    mm = offs_m < M
    cm = offs_c < C
    mask = mm[:, None] & cm[None, :]

    x = tl.load(x_ptr + offs_m[:, None] * C + offs_c[None, :], mask=mask, other=0.0).to(tl.float32)
    w = tl.load(w_ptr + offs_c, mask=cm, other=0.0).to(tl.float32)
    b = tl.load(b_ptr + offs_c, mask=cm, other=0.0).to(tl.float32)

    mean = tl.sum(x, axis=1) / C
    var = tl.sum(x * x, axis=1) / C - mean * mean
    var = tl.maximum(var, 0.0)
    rstd = 1.0 / tl.sqrt(var + eps)

    y = (x - mean[:, None]) * rstd[:, None] * w[None, :] + b[None, :]
    tl.store(y_ptr + offs_m[:, None] * C + offs_c[None, :], y.to(tl.bfloat16), mask=mask)


@triton.jit
def _precompute_tri_kernel(
    x_ptr,
    mask_ptr,
    wl_ptr,
    wr_ptr,
    wlg_ptr,
    wrg_ptr,
    wog_ptr,
    norm_w_ptr,
    norm_b_ptr,
    L_ptr,
    R_ptr,
    G_ptr,
    N,
    C: tl.constexpr,
    eps,
    H: tl.constexpr,
    P: tl.constexpr,
    BC: tl.constexpr,
    HAS_MASK: tl.constexpr,
):
    pid_k = tl.program_id(0)
    i = tl.program_id(1)
    b = tl.program_id(2)

    offs_k = pid_k * P + tl.arange(0, P)
    k_mask = offs_k < N

    x_base = x_ptr + (b * N * N + i * N) * C

    csum = tl.zeros((P,), dtype=tl.float32)
    cssq = tl.zeros((P,), dtype=tl.float32)

    for c0 in range(0, C, BC):
        offs_c = c0 + tl.arange(0, BC)
        cmask = (offs_c[:, None] < C) & (k_mask[None, :])
        xchunk = tl.load(
            x_base + offs_k[None, :] * C + offs_c[:, None],
            mask=cmask,
            other=0.0,
        ).to(tl.float32)
        csum += tl.sum(xchunk, axis=0)
        cssq += tl.sum(xchunk * xchunk, axis=0)

    mean = csum / C
    var = cssq / C - mean * mean
    var = tl.maximum(var, 0.0)
    rstd = 1.0 / tl.sqrt(var + eps)

    h_offs = tl.arange(0, H)

    accL = tl.zeros((H, P), dtype=tl.float32)
    accR = tl.zeros((H, P), dtype=tl.float32)
    accLG = tl.zeros((H, P), dtype=tl.float32)
    accRG = tl.zeros((H, P), dtype=tl.float32)
    accOG = tl.zeros((H, P), dtype=tl.float32)

    for c0 in range(0, C, BC):
        offs_c = c0 + tl.arange(0, BC)
        cmask = (offs_c[:, None] < C) & (k_mask[None, :])
        xchunk = tl.load(
            x_base + offs_k[None, :] * C + offs_c[:, None],
            mask=cmask,
            other=0.0,
        ).to(tl.float32)

        nw = tl.load(norm_w_ptr + offs_c, mask=offs_c < C, other=0.0).to(tl.float32)
        nb = tl.load(norm_b_ptr + offs_c, mask=offs_c < C, other=0.0).to(tl.float32)

        xnorm = (xchunk - mean[None, :]) * rstd[None, :] * nw[None, :] + nb[None, :]
        xnorm = tl.where(k_mask[None, :], xnorm, 0.0)
        xb = xnorm.to(tl.bfloat16)

        wmask = (h_offs[:, None] < H) & (offs_c[None, :] < C)

        wl = tl.load(wl_ptr + h_offs[:, None] * C + offs_c[None, :], mask=wmask, other=0.0).to(tl.bfloat16)
        wr = tl.load(wr_ptr + h_offs[:, None] * C + offs_c[None, :], mask=wmask, other=0.0).to(tl.bfloat16)
        wlg = tl.load(wlg_ptr + h_offs[:, None] * C + offs_c[None, :], mask=wmask, other=0.0).to(tl.bfloat16)
        wrg = tl.load(wrg_ptr + h_offs[:, None] * C + offs_c[None, :], mask=wmask, other=0.0).to(tl.bfloat16)
        wog = tl.load(wog_ptr + h_offs[:, None] * C + offs_c[None, :], mask=wmask, other=0.0).to(tl.bfloat16)

        accL = accL + tl.dot(wl, xb)
        accR = accR + tl.dot(wr, xb)
        accLG = accLG + tl.dot(wlg, xb)
        accRG = accRG + tl.dot(wrg, xb)
        accOG = accOG + tl.dot(wog, xb)

    if HAS_MASK:
        m = tl.load(mask_ptr + b * N * N + i * N + offs_k, mask=k_mask, other=0.0).to(tl.float32)
    else:
        m = tl.full((P,), 1.0, tl.float32)

    m = m[None, :]

    L = accL * tl.sigmoid(accLG) * m
    R = accR * tl.sigmoid(accRG) * m
    OG = tl.sigmoid(accOG)

    L_base = L_ptr + (b * H * N * N + i * N)
    L_ptrs = L_base + h_offs[:, None] * (N * N) + offs_k[None, :]
    tl.store(L_ptrs, L.to(tl.bfloat16), mask=k_mask[None, :])

    R_base = R_ptr + (b * H * N * N + i * N)
    R_ptrs = R_base + h_offs[:, None] * (N * N) + offs_k[None, :]
    tl.store(R_ptrs, R.to(tl.bfloat16), mask=k_mask[None, :])

    OG_T = tl.trans(OG).to(tl.bfloat16)
    G_base = G_ptr + (b * N * N + i * N) * H
    G_ptrs = G_base + offs_k[:, None] * H + h_offs[None, :]
    tl.store(G_ptrs, OG_T, mask=k_mask[:, None])


@triton.jit
def _final_single_kernel(
    out_ptr,
    gate_ptr,
    lw_ptr,
    lb_ptr,
    wout_ptr,
    y_ptr,
    M,
    H: tl.constexpr,
    C: tl.constexpr,
    eps,
    BLOCK_M: tl.constexpr,
    BLOCK_H: tl.constexpr,
    BLOCK_C: tl.constexpr,
):
    pid = tl.program_id(0)
    offs_m = pid * BLOCK_M + tl.arange(0, BLOCK_M)
    offs_h = tl.arange(0, BLOCK_H)

    mm = offs_m < M
    hm = offs_h < H
    omask = mm[:, None] & hm[None, :]

    out = tl.load(out_ptr + offs_m[:, None] * H + offs_h[None, :], mask=omask, other=0.0).to(tl.float32)
    gate = tl.load(gate_ptr + offs_m[:, None] * H + offs_h[None, :], mask=omask, other=0.0).to(tl.float32)

    lw = tl.load(lw_ptr + offs_h, mask=hm, other=0.0).to(tl.float32)
    lb = tl.load(lb_ptr + offs_h, mask=hm, other=0.0).to(tl.float32)

    mean = tl.sum(out, axis=1) / H
    var = tl.sum(out * out, axis=1) / H - mean * mean
    var = tl.maximum(var, 0.0)
    rstd = 1.0 / tl.sqrt(var + eps)

    x = (out - mean[:, None]) * rstd[:, None] * lw[None, :] + lb[None, :]
    x = x * gate
    x = x * hm[None, :].to(tl.float32)

    for c0 in range(0, C, BLOCK_C):
        offs_c = c0 + tl.arange(0, BLOCK_C)
        cm = offs_c < C
        wmask = hm[:, None] & cm[None, :]

        w = tl.load(
            wout_ptr + offs_c[None, :] * H + offs_h[:, None],
            mask=wmask,
            other=0.0,
        ).to(tl.float32)

        y = tl.dot(x, w, input_precision="tf32")
        tl.store(y_ptr + offs_m[:, None] * C + offs_c[None, :], y, mask=mm[:, None] & cm[None, :])


@triton.jit
def _final_chunk_kernel(
    out_ptr,
    gate_ptr,
    lw_ptr,
    lb_ptr,
    wout_ptr,
    y_ptr,
    M,
    H: tl.constexpr,
    C: tl.constexpr,
    eps,
    BLOCK_M: tl.constexpr,
    BLOCK_H: tl.constexpr,
    BLOCK_C: tl.constexpr,
):
    pid = tl.program_id(0)
    offs_m = pid * BLOCK_M + tl.arange(0, BLOCK_M)
    mm = offs_m < M

    csum = tl.zeros((BLOCK_M,), dtype=tl.float32)
    cssq = tl.zeros((BLOCK_M,), dtype=tl.float32)

    for h0 in range(0, H, BLOCK_H):
        offs_h = h0 + tl.arange(0, BLOCK_H)
        hm = offs_h < H
        mask = mm[:, None] & hm[None, :]

        val = tl.load(out_ptr + offs_m[:, None] * H + offs_h[None, :], mask=mask, other=0.0).to(tl.float32)
        csum += tl.sum(val, axis=1)
        cssq += tl.sum(val * val, axis=1)

    mean = csum / H
    var = cssq / H - mean * mean
    var = tl.maximum(var, 0.0)
    rstd = 1.0 / tl.sqrt(var + eps)

    for c0 in range(0, C, BLOCK_C):
        offs_c = c0 + tl.arange(0, BLOCK_C)
        cm = offs_c < C
        acc = tl.zeros((BLOCK_M, BLOCK_C), dtype=tl.float32)

        for h0 in range(0, H, BLOCK_H):
            offs_h = h0 + tl.arange(0, BLOCK_H)
            hm = offs_h < H
            mask = mm[:, None] & hm[None, :]

            val = tl.load(out_ptr + offs_m[:, None] * H + offs_h[None, :], mask=mask, other=0.0).to(tl.float32)
            gate = tl.load(gate_ptr + offs_m[:, None] * H + offs_h[None, :], mask=mask, other=0.0).to(tl.float32)
            lw = tl.load(lw_ptr + offs_h, mask=hm, other=0.0).to(tl.float32)
            lb = tl.load(lb_ptr + offs_h, mask=hm, other=0.0).to(tl.float32)

            x = (val - mean[:, None]) * rstd[:, None] * lw[None, :] + lb[None, :]
            x = x * gate
            x = x * hm[None, :].to(tl.float32)

            wmask = hm[:, None] & cm[None, :]
            w = tl.load(
                wout_ptr + offs_c[None, :] * H + offs_h[:, None],
                mask=wmask,
                other=0.0,
            ).to(tl.float32)

            acc = acc + tl.dot(x, w, input_precision="tf32")

        tl.store(y_ptr + offs_m[:, None] * C + offs_c[None, :], acc, mask=mm[:, None] & cm[None, :])


def _ln_to_bf16(x2d, C, weight, bias, device):
    M = x2d.shape[0]
    y = torch.empty((M, C), dtype=torch.bfloat16, device=device)

    BLOCK_C = triton.next_power_of_two(C)
    if BLOCK_C <= 128:
        BLOCK_M = 128
    elif BLOCK_C <= 256:
        BLOCK_M = 64
    elif BLOCK_C <= 512:
        BLOCK_M = 32
    else:
        BLOCK_M = 16

    weight = weight.to(torch.float32, device=device).contiguous()
    bias = bias.to(torch.float32, device=device).contiguous()

    grid = (triton.cdiv(M, BLOCK_M),)
    _ln_to_bf16_kernel[grid](
        x2d,
        y,
        weight,
        bias,
        M,
        C=C,
        eps=1e-5,
        BLOCK_M=BLOCK_M,
        BLOCK_C=BLOCK_C,
        num_warps=4,
    )
    return y


def _precompute_torch_separate(x, mask, weights, B, N, C, H, device):
    M = B * N * N
    dt = torch.bfloat16

    x2d = x.reshape(M, C)
    if not x2d.is_contiguous():
        x2d = x2d.contiguous()

    x_bf = _ln_to_bf16(
        x2d,
        C,
        weights["norm.weight"],
        weights["norm.bias"],
        device,
    )

    def wt(name):
        return weights[name].to(dtype=dt, device=device).t().contiguous()

    left = torch.matmul(x_bf, wt("left_proj.weight"))
    lg = torch.matmul(x_bf, wt("left_gate.weight"))
    torch.sigmoid_(lg)
    torch.mul_(left, lg)
    del lg

    right = torch.matmul(x_bf, wt("right_proj.weight"))
    rg = torch.matmul(x_bf, wt("right_gate.weight"))
    torch.sigmoid_(rg)
    torch.mul_(right, rg)
    del rg

    og = torch.matmul(x_bf, wt("out_gate.weight"))
    torch.sigmoid_(og)

    del x_bf

    if mask is not None:
        m = mask.to(dtype=dt, device=device).reshape(M, 1)
        torch.mul_(left, m)
        torch.mul_(right, m)

    Lh = left.view(B, N, N, H).permute(0, 3, 1, 2).contiguous()
    Rh = right.view(B, N, N, H).permute(0, 3, 1, 2).contiguous()

    del left, right
    return Lh, Rh, og


def custom_kernel(data):
    """
    Implement outgoing TriMul forward pass.

    Input data is (x, mask, weights, config) or (x, weights, config),
    with x: [B, N, N, C], mask: [B, N, N] (optional), weights: dict,
    config: dict containing dim and hidden_dim.
    """
    if len(data) == 4:
        x, mask, weights, config = data
    elif len(data) == 3:
        if isinstance(data[1], dict):
            x, weights, config = data
            mask = None
        else:
            x, mask, weights = data
            config = {}
    else:
        raise ValueError("Unexpected data layout.")

    device = x.device
    if not x.is_contiguous():
        x = x.contiguous()

    B, N, _, C = x.shape

    H = int(config.get("hidden_dim", weights["left_proj.weight"].shape[0]))
    M = B * N * N
    eps = 1e-5
    dt = torch.bfloat16

    if mask is not None:
        mask = mask.to(device=device)
        if mask.dim() == 4 and mask.shape[-1] == 1:
            mask = mask.reshape(B, N, N)
        mask = mask.contiguous()

    norm_w = weights["norm.weight"].to(torch.float32, device=device).contiguous()
    norm_b = weights["norm.bias"].to(torch.float32, device=device).contiguous()

    to_out_w = weights["to_out.weight"].to(torch.float32, device=device).contiguous()
    to_out_norm_w = weights["to_out_norm.weight"].to(torch.float32, device=device).contiguous()
    to_out_norm_b = weights["to_out_norm.bias"].to(torch.float32, device=device).contiguous()

    if H == 128:
        wl = weights["left_proj.weight"].to(dtype=dt, device=device).contiguous()
        wr = weights["right_proj.weight"].to(dtype=dt, device=device).contiguous()
        wlg = weights["left_gate.weight"].to(dtype=dt, device=device).contiguous()
        wrg = weights["right_gate.weight"].to(dtype=dt, device=device).contiguous()
        wog = weights["out_gate.weight"].to(dtype=dt, device=device).contiguous()

        Lh = torch.empty((B, H, N, N), dtype=dt, device=device)
        Rh = torch.empty((B, H, N, N), dtype=dt, device=device)
        G = torch.empty((M, H), dtype=dt, device=device)

        P = 32
        BC = 128
        grid = (triton.cdiv(N, P), N, B)

        mask_arg = mask if mask is not None else x

        _precompute_tri_kernel[grid](
            x,
            mask_arg,
            wl,
            wr,
            wlg,
            wrg,
            wog,
            norm_w,
            norm_b,
            Lh,
            Rh,
            G,
            N,
            C=C,
            eps=eps,
            H=H,
            P=P,
            BC=BC,
            HAS_MASK=(mask is not None),
            num_warps=8,
        )

        del wl, wr, wlg, wrg, wog
    else:
        Lh, Rh, G = _precompute_torch_separate(
            x,
            mask,
            weights,
            B,
            N,
            C,
            H,
            device,
        )

    LH = Lh.view(B * H, N, N)
    RH = Rh.view(B * H, N, N)
    out_h = torch.bmm(LH, RH.transpose(1, 2))

    del Lh, Rh, LH, RH

    out = out_h.view(B, H, N, N).permute(0, 2, 3, 1).contiguous()
    del out_h

    y = torch.empty((B, N, N, C), dtype=torch.float32, device=device)

    if C >= 128:
        BLOCK_C = 128
    else:
        BLOCK_C = max(16, triton.next_power_of_two(C))

    BLOCK_M = 64
    grid = (triton.cdiv(M, BLOCK_M),)

    if H <= 128:
        BLOCK_H = max(16, triton.next_power_of_two(H))
        _final_single_kernel[grid](
            out,
            G,
            to_out_norm_w,
            to_out_norm_b,
            to_out_w,
            y,
            M,
            H=H,
            C=C,
            eps=eps,
            BLOCK_M=BLOCK_M,
            BLOCK_H=BLOCK_H,
            BLOCK_C=BLOCK_C,
            num_warps=4,
        )
    else:
        BLOCK_H = 128
        _final_chunk_kernel[grid](
            out,
            G,
            to_out_norm_w,
            to_out_norm_b,
            to_out_w,
            y,
            M,
            H=H,
            C=C,
            eps=eps,
            BLOCK_M=BLOCK_M,
            BLOCK_H=BLOCK_H,
            BLOCK_C=BLOCK_C,
            num_warps=4,
        )

    return y
```
